** DONE Dtrt Indent
Heuristic indentation detection for programming buffers. Executes post-mode initialization to preempt LSP spawning and aborts on massive files.
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(use-package dtrt-indent
  :defer t
  :custom
  ;; Defers to SMIE indentation engines in supported modes.
  (dtrt-indent-run-after-smie t)
  ;; Synchronizes Emacs' global fallback indentation variable.
  (dtrt-indent-update-standard-indent t)
  ;; Restricts line scanning to prevent main-thread micro-stutters.
  (dtrt-indent-max-lines 2000)
  ;; Minimum confidence threshold for indentation detection.
  (dtrt-indent-min-quality 80.0)
  ;; Suppresses echo-area logging.
  (dtrt-indent-verbosity 0)
  :init
  (defun ar/dtrt-indent-maybe-enable ()
    "Enable `dtrt-indent-mode' in programming buffers, excluding structural exceptions."
    (when (and (derived-mode-p 'prog-mode 'conf-mode)
               (not (too-long-file-p))
               ;; Excludes Makefiles (requires hard tabs) and YAML (strict spacing).
               (not (apply #'derived-mode-p '(makefile-mode yaml-mode))))
      (dtrt-indent-mode 1)))
  ;; Executes after major-mode body but before mode hooks and LSP spawning.
  (add-hook 'change-major-mode-after-body-hook #'ar/dtrt-indent-maybe-enable)
  :config
  ;; Synchronizes Evil shift-width with the detected indentation offset.
  (add-hook 'dtrt-indent-hook
            (lambda () (setq-local evil-shift-width tab-width))))
#+end_src

** DONE Indent Bars
AST-aware vertical guides synchronizing depth colorization with rainbow-delimiters. Restricts activation to programming buffers to prevent Org-mode structural corruption.
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(use-package indent-bars
  :defer t
  :init
  ;; Blocks activation in massive files, child frames, fontification buffers, and Org buffers.
  (defun ar/indent-bars-should-enable-p ()
    "Return non-nil if indent-bars should be enabled in current buffer."
    (not (or (too-long-file-p)
             (frame-parameter nil 'parent-frame)
             (bound-and-true-p org-indent-mode)
             (string-prefix-p " *org-src-fontification:" (buffer-name))
             (derived-mode-p 'org-mode
                             'lisp-mode
                             'emacs-lisp-mode
                             'lisp-interaction-mode 'lisp-data-mode))))
  (defun ar/indent-bars-maybe-enable ()
    "Enable indent-bars-mode if conditions are met."
    (when (ar/indent-bars-should-enable-p)
      (indent-bars-mode 1)))
  :custom
  ;; Enforces AST structural boundaries and prevents string descent.
  (indent-bars-treesit-support t)
  (indent-bars-no-descend-lists t)
  (indent-bars-no-descend-string 'all)
  ;; Encapsulates multi-line AST nodes to prevent visual fragmentation.
  (indent-bars-treesit-wrap '((python arguments parameters argument_list parameter_list)
                              (c argument_list parameter_list)
                              (cpp argument_list parameter_list)
                              (rust arguments parameters)
                              (javascript arguments formal_parameters)
                              (typescript arguments formal_parameters)
                              (tsx arguments formal_parameters)))
  ;; Renders ghost bars on empty lines to connect structural blocks.
  (indent-bars-display-on-blank-lines t)
  ;; Unicode fallback for terminals and PGTK stipple failures.
  (indent-bars-no-stipple-char ?▍)
  ;; Tokyo Night depth palette matching rainbow-delimiters.
  (indent-bars-color-by-depth '(:palette ("#7aa2f7" "#e0af68" "#9ece6a" "#1abc9c" "#bb9af7" "#9d7cd8" "#ff9e64" "#f7768e") :blend 1))
  ;; High-contrast active scope tracking.
  (indent-bars-highlight-current-depth '(:color "#c0caf5" :blend 0.3 :width 0.25))
  :hook ((prog-mode conf-mode yaml-mode) . ar/indent-bars-maybe-enable)
  :config
  ;; Disables rendering inside magit-blame overlays.
  (with-eval-after-load 'magit-blame
    (add-to-list 'magit-blame-disable-modes 'indent-bars-mode)))
#+end_src

** DONE Move Text
Provides O(1) line and region dragging. Automatically re-indents displaced lines to preserve structural integrity in whitespace-sensitive languages.
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(use-package move-text
  :defer t
  :commands (move-text-up move-text-down)
  :config
  ;; Re-indent the displaced line or region to preserve structural integrity.
  (defun ar/move-text-indent-region-advice (&rest ignored)
    "Re-indent the current line or active region after moving text."
    (let ((deactivate deactivate-mark))
      (if (region-active-p)
          (indent-region (region-beginning) (region-end))
        (indent-region (line-beginning-position) (line-end-position)))
      (setq deactivate-mark deactivate)))
  (advice-add #'move-text-up :after #'ar/move-text-indent-region-advice)
  (advice-add #'move-text-down :after #'ar/move-text-indent-region-advice))
#+end_src

(use-package display-line-numbers
  :ensure nil
  :defer t
  :hook ((prog-mode conf-mode yaml-mode LaTeX-mode tex-mode) . display-line-numbers-mode)
  :custom
  ;; Pre-calculates maximum margin width to prevent initial rendering shifts.
  (display-line-numbers-width-start t)
  ;; Prevents margin shrinking to eliminate horizontal redisplay jitter.
  (display-line-numbers-grow-only t)
  ;; Hybrid paradigm: absolute current line, relative surrounding lines.
  (display-line-numbers-type 'relative)
  :custom-face
  ;; Recessive margin typography to prevent visual fatigue.
  (line-number ((t (:inherit shadow :foreground "#565f89"))))
  ;; High-contrast active line tracking.
  (line-number-current-line ((t (:inherit default :foreground "#c0caf5" :weight bold))))
  :config
  ;; Aborts activation in massive buffers.
  (define-advice display-line-numbers-mode (:before-while (&optional arg) guard-large-files)
    (or (and arg (< (prefix-numeric-value arg) 1))
        (not (too-long-file-p)))))

** DONE Active Line Highlight

#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(use-package hl-line
  :ensure nil
  :custom
  ;; Confines highlight strictly to the active window.
  (hl-line-sticky-flag nil)
  ;; Forces overlay behind syntax highlighting, diagnostics, and whitespace markers.
  (hl-line-overlay-priority -1)
  :config
  ;; Intercepts the render cycle to delete the overlay in excluded modes.
  (define-advice global-hl-line-highlight (:around (fn) guard-excluded-modes)
    (if (derived-mode-p 'dired-mode
                        'magit-mode
                        'compilation-mode
                        'special-mode
                        'dashboard-mode
                        'eshell-mode
                        'shell-mode
                        'term-mode
                        'vterm-mode
                        'eat-mode
                        'ghostel-mode
                        'ghostel-compile-view-mode
                        'pdf-view-mode
                        'treemacs-mode)
        (when global-hl-line-overlay (delete-overlay global-hl-line-overlay))
      (funcall fn)))
  ;; Destroys the overlay on Evil visual state entry.
  (add-hook 'evil-visual-state-entry-hook #'global-hl-line-unhighlight)
  ;; Redraws the overlay upon exiting Evil visual state.
  (add-hook 'evil-visual-state-exit-hook #'global-hl-line-highlight)
  ;; Activates synchronously to guarantee overlay registration.
  (global-hl-line-mode 1))

** DONE Modeline
Minimalist modeline engine featuring native Evil state tracking, IDE synergy, and prose word-count integration. Redundant native indicators are disabled to preserve horizontal space and prevent variable inheritance collisions.
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(use-package doom-modeline
  :init
  ;; Disables native size indicators and percentage formats before package load
  ;; to prevent doom-modeline from inheriting and rendering redundant segments.
  (size-indication-mode -1)
  (setq-default mode-line-percent-position nil)
  :custom
  ;; Visual dimensions and split-window overflow safeguard.
  (doom-modeline-height 25)
  (doom-modeline-bar-width 3)
  (doom-modeline-window-width-limit 90)
  ;; Project detection safely falls back to native `project.el'.
  (doom-modeline-project-detection 'auto)
  (doom-modeline-buffer-file-name-style 'relative-from-project)
  ;; Disables redundant size, encoding, and percentage indicators.
  (doom-modeline-buffer-file-size nil)
  (doom-modeline-buffer-encoding nil)
  (doom-modeline-percent-position nil)
  ;; Enables native Evil state, modal icons, and major-mode glyphs.
  (doom-modeline-modal t)
  (doom-modeline-modal-icon t)
  (doom-modeline-major-mode-icon t)
  ;; IDE synergy: LSP server status and active language version rendering.
  (doom-modeline-lsp t)
  (doom-modeline-env-version t)
  ;; Version control integration.
  (doom-modeline-vcs-icon t)
  (doom-modeline-vcs-max-length 0)
  ;; Prose word-count engine strictly confined to writing modes.
  (doom-modeline-enable-word-count t)
  (doom-modeline-continuous-word-count-modes '(markdown-mode gfm-mode org-mode))
  ;; Disables unused integrations to prevent background I/O overhead.
  (doom-modeline-github nil)
  (doom-modeline-mu4e nil)

** DONE Hide Modeline
Native redisplay concealment for terminal, agenda, and UI buffers. Replaces unmaintained dependencies with a zero-overhead buffer-local minor mode.
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(defvar-local ar/saved-mode-line-format nil "Cached modeline format.")
(defvar-local ar/saved-header-line-format nil "Cached header-line format.")
(defvar-local ar/saved-tab-line-format nil "Cached tab-line format.")

(define-minor-mode ar/hide-modeline-mode
  "Conceal the modeline, header-line, and tab-line in specific buffers."
  :init-value nil
  :lighter nil
  (if ar/hide-modeline-mode
      (progn
        ;; Cache original formats only if they are not already nil.
        (unless (eq mode-line-format nil)
          (setq-local ar/saved-mode-line-format mode-line-format))
        (unless (eq header-line-format nil)
          (setq-local ar/saved-header-line-format header-line-format))
        (unless (eq tab-line-format nil)
          (setq-local ar/saved-tab-line-format tab-line-format))
        ;; Force concealment.
        (setq-local mode-line-format nil
                    header-line-format nil
                    tab-line-format nil))
    ;; Restore cached formats upon deactivation.
    (setq-local mode-line-format ar/saved-mode-line-format
                header-line-format ar/saved-header-line-format
                tab-line-format ar/saved-tab-line-format))
  ;; Forces C-level redisplay to prevent phantom spacing artifacts.
  (force-mode-line-update))

(defun ar/enable-hide-modeline ()
  "Safely enable `ar/hide-modeline-mode' without toggling."
  (ar/hide-modeline-mode 1))

(dolist (mode '(eat-mode eshell-mode shell-mode term-mode vterm-mode
                embark-collect-mode lsp-ui-imenu-mode ghostel-mode
                ghostel-compile-view-mode pdf-annot-list-mode))
  (add-hook (intern (format "%s-hook" mode)) #'ar/enable-hide-modeline))

;; Org-agenda aggressively resets the modeline during finalization.
(add-hook 'org-agenda-finalize-hook #'ar/enable-hide-modeline)
#+end_src

;; Lowers the zoom multiplier for granular scaling steps.

;; Hardcoded arguments bypass the interactive prefix parser to prevent sign inversion.

  (interactive)
  (global-text-scale-adjust 1))

(defun ar/global-zoom-out ()
  "Decrease global text scale across all frames."

  (global-text-scale-adjust -1))

** DONE Avy
Provides an O(1) radix-tree jumping engine for long-distance spatial navigation. Configured with the =at-full= overlay style, home-row routing, proximity sorting, and on-demand multi-window expansion.
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(use-package avy
  :defer t
  :commands (avy-goto-char-2
             avy-goto-line
             avy-goto-word-1
             avy-goto-word-0
             avy-isearch
             avy-move-line
             avy-copy-line
             avy-kill-region
             avy-transpose-lines-in-region
             avy-move-region
             avy-copy-region
             avy-kill-ring-save-region)
  :bind (:map isearch-mode-map
         ("M-j" . avy-isearch))
  :custom
  ;; Path Generation: Standard shortest-path algorithm restricted to home-row.
  (avy-keys '(?a ?s ?d ?f ?g ?h ?j ?k ?l))
  ;; Visual Rendering: `at-full` preserves peripheral word context.
  (avy-style 'at-full)
  ;; Buffer Dimming: Forces focus strictly to the opaque neon spotlights.
  (avy-background t)
  ;; Scope Control: Defaults to active window; `C-u` expands search to all windows.
  (avy-all-windows nil)
  (avy-all-windows-alt t)
  ;; Candidate Validation: Renders overlay for single targets to prevent accidental jumps.
  (avy-single-candidate-jump nil)
  ;; Proximity Engine: Sorts candidates by physical distance to cursor.
  (avy-orders-alist '((avy-goto-char . avy-order-closest)
                      (avy-goto-word-0 . avy-order-closest)
                      (avy-goto-word-1 . avy-order-closest)))
  ;; Input Latency: Tightens the seek-then-jump response time.
  (avy-timeout-seconds 0.3)
  :custom-face
  ;; Opaque Spotlight Faces: 100% opaque backgrounds prevent ghosting artifacts.
  (avy-lead-face-0 ((t (:background "#f7768e" :foreground "#1a1b26" :weight bold))))
  (avy-lead-face-1 ((t (:background "#e0af68" :foreground "#1a1b26" :weight bold))))
  (avy-lead-face-2 ((t (:background "#9ece6a" :foreground "#1a1b26" :weight bold))))
  (avy-lead-face ((t (:background "#7dcfff" :foreground "#1a1b26" :weight bold)))))

** DONE Evil Easymotion
Wraps Avy into Evil's operator-pending grammar via a unified =gs= prefix. Bypasses =evilem-create= infinite loop traps by registering native Avy commands directly into Evil's C-level motion registry.
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(use-package evil-easymotion
  :after (evil avy)
  :config
  ;; Native Evil motion wrappers bypass `evilem-create` UI traps.
  (evil-define-motion ar/evil-avy-goto-char-2 ()
    "Jump to 2-char target."
    :type exclusive :jump t
    (avy-goto-char-2))
  (evil-define-motion ar/evil-avy-goto-line ()
    "Jump to visible line."
    :type line :jump t
    (avy-goto-line))
  (evil-define-motion ar/evil-avy-goto-char-timer ()
    "Jump to string target via timer."
    :type exclusive :jump t
    (avy-goto-char-timer))
  ;; Populate `evilem-map` under the "gs" prefix.
  (evilem-default-keybindings "gs")
  ;; Inject custom wrappers into the `gs` namespace.
  (define-key evilem-map "s" #'ar/evil-avy-goto-char-2)
  (define-key evilem-map "l" #'ar/evil-avy-goto-line)
  (define-key evilem-map "SPC" #'ar/evil-avy-goto-char-timer))

** DONE Evil Snipe
Replaces native Evil character motions with incremental, regex-aware spatial jumps. Confines activation to text buffers while protecting specialized UI and terminal modes from keymap collisions.
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(use-package evil-snipe
  :after evil
  :custom
  (evil-snipe-enable-highlight t)
  (evil-snipe-enable-incremental-highlight t)
  (evil-snipe-auto-disable-substitute t)
  ;; Full-screen spatial awareness for incremental highlighting.
  (evil-snipe-scope 'whole-visible)
  ;; Vim parity: lowercase is case-insensitive, uppercase is strict.
  (evil-snipe-smart-case t)
  ;; Keeps the echo area reserved for ElDoc and Which-Key.
  (evil-snipe-show-prompt nil)
  ;; Follows the cursor when jumping outside the current viewport.
  (evil-snipe-auto-scroll t)
  ;; Expands search scope to the entire buffer if a localized snipe fails.
  (evil-snipe-spillover-scope 'whole-buffer)
  ;; Allows pressing TAB to dynamically increase the snipe buffer size.
  (evil-snipe-tab-increment t)
  :custom-face
  (evil-snipe-first-match-face ((t (:foreground "#7dcfff" :underline t))))
  (evil-snipe-matches-face ((t (:foreground "#7dcfff" :underline t))))
  :config
  ;; Populate exclusions and aliases BEFORE activating the global modes.
  ;; Modifying `defcustom` lists must happen in `:config` to prevent upstream overwrites.
  (dolist (mode '(pdf-view-mode
                  dirvish-directory-view-mode
                  dirvish-mode
                  git-timemachine-mode
                  vterm-mode
                  eat-mode
                  eshell-mode
                  ghostel-mode
                  term-mode
                  compilation-mode
                  grep-mode
                  occur-mode
                  deadgrep-mode
                  rg-mode
                  ag-mode
                  flycheck-error-list-mode
                  flymake-diagnostics-buffer-mode
                  xref--xref-buffer-mode
                  embark-collect-mode
                  lsp-ui-imenu-mode
                  help-mode
                  helpful-mode
                  Info-mode
                  woman-mode
                  eww-mode
                  docker-container-mode
                  docker-image-mode
                  Custom-mode
                  ediff-mode
                  profiler-report-mode
                  dashboard-mode
                  messages-buffer-mode
                  backtrace-mode))
    (add-to-list 'evil-snipe-disabled-modes mode))
  ;; Map brackets and quotes to regex patterns for AST-boundary jumping.
  (push '(?\[ "[[{(<]") evil-snipe-aliases)
  (push '(?\] "[]})>]") evil-snipe-aliases)
  (push '(?\" "[\"'`´]") evil-snipe-aliases)
  ;; Activate global modes AFTER the exclusion list is fully populated.
  (evil-snipe-mode 1)
  (evil-snipe-override-mode 1)
  ;; Visual State Bindings: Inject `z` and `Z` to allow initiating a snipe and capturing the traversed region.
  (evil-define-key 'visual evil-snipe-local-mode-map "z" #'evil-snipe-s)
  (evil-define-key 'visual evil-snipe-local-mode-map "Z" #'evil-snipe-S)
  ;; Inject `evilem-map` into the snipe parent transient map.
  (with-eval-after-load 'evil-easymotion
    (define-key evil-snipe-parent-transient-map "g" evilem-map)))
#+end_src

** DONE Grep
Finds text across files and allows direct editing of the search results to update the original files.
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(use-package grep
  :ensure nil
  :commands (grep
             lgrep
             rgrep
             zgrep
             zrgrep)
  :custom
  ;; Prevents automatic buffer saving before search execution.
  (grep-save-buffers nil)
  ;; Groups search results by filename with visual separators.
  (grep-use-headings t)
  :config
  ;; `grep-apply-setting` persists templates across `grep-compute-defaults` cycles.
  (when-let ((rg (executable-find "rg")))
    (grep-apply-setting 'grep-command (format "%s -nH --null --no-heading --color=never -e " rg))
    ;; `<X>` and `<C>` are dropped; `rg` lacks `--exclude` and ANSI colors corrupt `compilation-mode`.
    (grep-apply-setting 'grep-template
                        (format "%s -nH --null --no-heading --color=never -e <R> <F>" rg))
    (grep-apply-setting 'grep-find-command (format "%s -nH --null --no-heading --color=never -e " rg))
    ;; Routes `<X>` to `find` to bypass `rg`'s missing `--exclude` flag, then `-exec`s into `rg`.
    (grep-apply-setting 'grep-find-template
                        (format "find <D> <X> -type f <F> -exec %s -nH --null --no-heading --color=never -e <R> {} +" rg))
    ;; Routes `xref-find-references` natively to ripgrep's regex engine.
    (setq xref-search-program 'ripgrep))
  ;; Forces Evil into normal state for read-only navigation.
  (evil-set-initial-state 'grep-mode 'normal)
  ;; Forces Evil into normal state for the writable edit mode.
  (evil-set-initial-state 'grep-edit-mode 'normal)
  ;; Injects Vim "write and quit" / "quit without saving" parity for grep mutations.
  (evil-define-key 'normal grep-edit-mode-map
    "ZZ" #'grep-edit-save
    "ZQ" #'grep-edit-abort))
#+end_src




** DONE Re-Builder
Provides an interactive visual workspace to construct, test, and highlight complex search patterns against live buffer text before execution.
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(use-package re-builder
  :ensure nil
  :commands (re-builder
             regexp-builder)
  :custom
  ;; Eliminates double-escaping ("backslash hell") for external tool parity.
  (reb-re-syntax 'string)
  ;; Caps overlay rendering to prevent main-thread freezing on large buffers.
  (reb-auto-match-limit 2000)
  :config
  ;; Guarantees immediate typing upon invocation without requiring state transition.
  (evil-set-initial-state 'reb-mode 'insert)
  ;; Guarantees immediate typing when switching to `read` or `rx` syntax.
  (when (boundp 'reb-lisp-mode)
    (evil-set-initial-state 'reb-lisp-mode 'insert))
  ;; Strips Elisp string quotes/escapes for direct use in `query-replace-regexp` or CLI tools.
  (defun ar/re-builder-copy-interactive ()
    "Copy current regex to kill-ring without Elisp string escaping."
    (interactive)
    (reb-copy)
    (let* ((raw (pop kill-ring))
           (clean (replace-regexp-in-string
                   (rx "\\" (group anything)) "\\1"
                   (string-trim raw "\"" "\""))))
      (kill-new clean)
      (message "Copied interactive regex: %s" clean)))
  ;; Executes `occur` against the target buffer using the constructed regex.
  (defun ar/re-builder-occur ()
    "Run `occur' with the current regex in the target buffer."
    (interactive)
    ;; Defensive guard against killed target buffers.
    (unless (buffer-live-p reb-target-buffer)
      (user-error "Target buffer no longer exists"))
    (let* ((target reb-target-buffer)
           ;; Canonical upstream macro for safe regex extraction.
           (regex (reb-target-binding reb-regexp)))
      (reb-quit)
      (switch-to-buffer target)
      (occur regex)))
  ;; Injects custom Transient menu for unified regex manipulation.
  (with-eval-after-load 'transient
    (defun ar/re-builder-title ()
      (concat
       (if (fboundp 'nerd-icons-mdicon)
           (nerd-icons-mdicon "nf-md-regex" :face 'transient-heading :v-adjust 0.02)
         "")
       (propertize (format " Regex Builder (%s)"
                           (if (bufferp reb-target-buffer)
                               (buffer-name reb-target-buffer)
                             "No Target"))
                   'face 'transient-heading)))
    (transient-define-prefix ar/re-builder-transient ()
      "Regex Builder Control Panel"
      [:description ar/re-builder-title
       ["Change"
        ("x" "Syntax" reb-change-syntax :transient t)
        ("b" "Target Buffer" reb-change-target-buffer :transient t)
        ("t" "Case Sensitivity" reb-toggle-case :transient t)]
       ["Match"
        ("n" "Next" reb-next-match :transient t)
        ("p" "Previous" reb-prev-match :transient t)
        ("f" "Force Update" reb-force-update :transient t)]
       ["Display"
        ("s" "Subexp Mode" reb-enter-subexp-mode)
        ("o" "Occur Target" ar/re-builder-occur)]
       ["Copy"
        ("c" "Code (Elisp)" reb-copy :transient t)
        ("w" "Interactive" ar/re-builder-copy-interactive :transient t)]
       ["Exit"
        ("q" "Quit Builder" reb-quit)
        ("<escape>" "" transient-quit-one :format " ")]]
    ;; Routes `C-o` to summon the Transient menu from within the builder buffer.
    (define-key reb-mode-map (kbd "C-o") #'ar/re-builder-transient)
    (when (boundp 'reb-lisp-mode-map)
      (define-key reb-lisp-mode-map (kbd "C-o") #'ar/re-builder-transient))))

;; Registers global leader binding for rapid workspace invocation.
(ar/global-leader
  "s x" '(re-builder :wk "Regex builder"))
#+end_src


** DONE Avy Transient Menu
Provides a centralized dashboard for Avy's spatial jumping and remote text mutation APIs.
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
;; DRY dispatcher for Avy scope modifiers.
(defun ar/avy-dispatch (cmd)
  "Execute Avy CMD with `-above` or `-below` suffix based on transient args."
  (let* ((t-args (transient-args transient-current-command))
         (above (member "--above" t-args))
         (below (member "--below" t-args))
         (suffix (cond ((and above below) "")
                       (above "-above")
                       (below "-below")
                       (t "")))
         (target (intern (concat (symbol-name cmd) suffix))))
    (call-interactively (if (fboundp target) target cmd))))

;; Explicit wrappers to satisfy Transient's command resolution.
(defun ar/avy-goto-char-2 () (interactive) (ar/avy-dispatch 'avy-goto-char-2))
(defun ar/avy-goto-word-1 () (interactive) (ar/avy-dispatch 'avy-goto-word-1))
(defun ar/avy-goto-symbol-1 () (interactive) (ar/avy-dispatch 'avy-goto-symbol-1))
(defun ar/avy-goto-whitespace-end () (interactive) (ar/avy-dispatch 'avy-goto-whitespace-end))
(defun ar/avy-goto-line () (interactive) (ar/avy-dispatch 'avy-goto-line))

(with-eval-after-load 'transient
  (defun ar/avy-title ()
    (concat
     (if (fboundp 'nerd-icons-faicon)
         (nerd-icons-faicon "nf-fa-crosshairs" :face 'transient-heading :v-adjust 0.02)
       "")
     (propertize " Avy Spatial Jumps & Mutations" 'face 'transient-heading)))

  (transient-define-prefix ar/avy-transient ()
    "Avy Spatial Jumps and Mutations Dashboard"
    [:description ar/avy-title
     ["Scope (applies to ⬍)"
      :description (lambda ()
                     (let ((args (transient-args transient-current-command)))
                       (format "Scope (applies to (%s))"
                               (cond ((and (member "--above" args) (member "--below" args)) "#")
                                     ((member "--above" args) "↑")
                                     ((member "--below" args) "↓")
                                     (t "#")))))
      :class transient-row
      ("a" "Above" "--above")
      ("b" "Below" "--below")]

     ["Goto Thing"
      ("c" "Character" avy-goto-char-timer)
      ("2" "2 Characters ⬍" ar/avy-goto-char-2)
      ("w" "Word ⬍" ar/avy-goto-word-1)
      ("s" "Symbol ⬍" ar/avy-goto-symbol-1)
      ("W" "Whitespace end ⬍" ar/avy-goto-whitespace-end)
      ("p" "Pop mark" avy-pop-mark)]

     ["Goto Line"
      :pad-keys t
      ("l" "Line ⬍" ar/avy-goto-line)
      ("e" "End of line" avy-goto-end-of-line)
      ("o" "Org heading" avy-org-goto-heading-timer :if (lambda () (derived-mode-p 'org-mode)))
      ("n" "Line number" goto-line :if (lambda () (bound-and-true-p display-line-numbers-mode)))]

     ["Edit Other Line"
      ("C" "Copy" avy-kill-ring-save-whole-line)
      ("k" "Kill" avy-kill-whole-line :if (lambda () (not buffer-read-only)))
      ("m" "Move" avy-move-line :if (lambda () (not buffer-read-only)))
      ("d" "Duplicate" avy-copy-line :if (lambda () (not buffer-read-only)))]

     ["Edit Other Region"
      ("r" "Copy" avy-kill-ring-save-region)
      ("K" "Kill" avy-kill-region :if (lambda () (not buffer-read-only)))
      ("M" "Move" avy-move-region :if (lambda () (not buffer-read-only)))
      ("D" "Duplicate" avy-copy-region :if (lambda () (not buffer-read-only)))
      ("t" "Transpose" avy-transpose-lines-in-region :if (lambda () (and (not buffer-read-only) (region-active-p))))]

     ["Index & Errors"
      ("g" "Org Goto…" org-goto :if (lambda () (derived-mode-p 'org-mode)))
      ("i" "Index…" imenu :if (lambda () (bound-and-true-p imenu--index-alist)))
      ("M-p" "Previous" previous-error)
      ("M-n" "Next" next-error)]

     ["Exit"
      ("<escape>" "" transient-quit-one :format " ")
      ("RET" "Exit Avy" transient-quit-all)]]))

;; Registers global leader category and bindings for rapid spatial invocation.
(ar/global-leader
  "y"   '(:ignore t :wk "avy/jump")
  "y y" '(ar/avy-transient :wk "Avy dashboard")
  "y c" '(avy-goto-char-timer :wk "Char timer")
  "y l" '(avy-goto-line :wk "Line")
  "y w" '(avy-goto-word-1 :wk "Word")
  "y s" '(avy-goto-symbol-1 :wk "Symbol"))
#+end_src

#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(use-package ediff
  :ensure nil
  :defer t
  :commands (ediff ediff-files ediff-buffers ediff-files3 ediff-buffers3
             ediff-revision ediff-current-file ediff-merge ediff-merge-with-ancestor
             ediff-patch-file ediff-patch-buffer)

  :init
  ;; Window and display configuration
  (setq ediff-window-setup-function 'ediff-setup-windows-plain
        ediff-split-window-function 'split-window-horizontally
        ediff-merge-split-window-function 'split-window-horizontally)

  ;; Start with minimal control panel
  (setq ediff-use-long-help-message nil)

  ;; Highlight options
  (setq ediff-highlight-all-diffs t)

  :config
  ;; Window configuration management
  (defvar ar/ediff-window-config nil
    "Window configuration before ediff.")

  (defun ar/ediff-save-window-config ()
    "Save current window configuration before ediff."
    (setq ar/ediff-window-config (current-window-configuration)))

  (defun ar/ediff-restore-window-config ()
    "Restore window configuration after ediff."
    (when ar/ediff-window-config
      (set-window-configuration ar/ediff-window-config)
      (setq ar/ediff-window-config nil)))

  (add-hook 'ediff-before-setup-hook #'ar/ediff-save-window-config)
  (add-hook 'ediff-quit-hook #'ar/ediff-restore-window-config)

  ;; Define transient menu
  (with-eval-after-load 'transient
    (defun ar/ediff-title ()
      (concat
       (if (fboundp 'nerd-icons-faicon)
           (nerd-icons-faicon "nf-fa-columns" :face 'transient-heading :v-adjust 0.02)
         "")
       (propertize " Ediff Control Panel" 'face 'transient-heading)))

    (transient-define-prefix ar/ediff-transient ()
      "Ediff commands - call from control panel"
      [:description ar/ediff-title

       ["Navigate"
        ("n" "next diff" ediff-next-difference :transient t)
        ("p" "previous diff" ediff-previous-difference :transient t)
        ("d" "jump to diff" ediff-jump-to-difference :transient t)
        ("g" "goto point" ediff-jump-to-difference-at-point :transient t)
        ("v" "scroll down" ediff-scroll-vertically :transient t)
        ("V" "scroll up" (lambda () (interactive) (ediff-scroll-vertically '-)) :transient t)
        ("<" "scroll left" ediff-scroll-horizontally :transient t)
        (">" "scroll right" (lambda () (interactive) (ediff-scroll-horizontally '-)) :transient t)]

       ["Copy & Restore"
        ("a" "copy A → B" ediff-copy-A-to-B :transient t)
        ("b" "copy B → A" ediff-copy-B-to-A :transient t)
        ("r" "restore diff" ediff-restore-diff :transient t)
        ("*" "refine region" ediff-make-or-kill-fine-diffs :transient t)]

       ["Toggle"
        ("h" "highlighting" ediff-toggle-hilit :transient t)
        ("@" "auto-refine" ediff-toggle-autorefine :transient t)
        ("#" "skip similar" ediff-toggle-skip-similar :transient t)
        ("|" "split H/V" ediff-toggle-split :transient t)
        ("m" "wide display" ediff-toggle-wide-display :transient t)]

       ["Actions"
        ("!" "update diffs" ediff-update-diffs :transient t)
        ("w" "save buffers" ediff-save-buffer :transient t)
        ("i" "status info" ediff-status-info :transient t)
        ("E" "manual" ediff-documentation :transient t)]

       ["Exit"
        ("?" "toggle help" ediff-toggle-help :transient t)
        ("z" "suspend" ediff-suspend)
        ("q" "quit" ediff-quit)
        ("<escape>" "" transient-quit-one :format " ")]])

    ;; Function to check if transient is active
    (defun ar/ediff-transient-active-p ()
      "Check if ediff transient is currently active."
      (and (boundp 'transient--prefix)
           transient--prefix
           (eq transient-current-command 'ar/ediff-transient))))

  ;; Setup custom keybindings in control panel
  (defun ar/ediff-setup-keys ()
    "Setup custom keybindings for ediff control panel.
This function unbinds SPC and other keys that conflict with general.el,
and sets up vim-style navigation."
    ;; CRITICAL: Unbind SPC to prevent conflicts with general.el leader key
    (define-key ediff-mode-map (kbd "SPC") nil)

    ;; Unbind other potentially conflicting keys
    (define-key ediff-mode-map (kbd "C-c") nil)

    ;; Vim-style navigation (j/k for next/previous)
    (define-key ediff-mode-map (kbd "j") 'ediff-next-difference)
    (define-key ediff-mode-map (kbd "k") 'ediff-previous-difference)
    (define-key ediff-mode-map (kbd "h") 'ediff-previous-difference)
    (define-key ediff-mode-map (kbd "l") 'ediff-next-difference)

    ;; Bind transient menu to ? (toggles between transient and help)
    (when (featurep 'transient)
      (define-key ediff-mode-map (kbd "?")
        (lambda ()
          (interactive)
          (if (ar/ediff-transient-active-p)
              (ediff-toggle-help)
            (ar/ediff-transient))))))

  (add-hook 'ediff-keymap-setup-hook #'ar/ediff-setup-keys)

  ;; Ensure control panel gets focus on startup
  (add-hook 'ediff-startup-hook
            (lambda ()
              (select-window (get-buffer-window ediff-control-buffer)))))
#+end_src














** TODO Woman
Extracts system =manpath= directories to guarantee native Emacs =woman= can locate modern Arch Linux manuals without external dependencies.
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(use-package woman
  :ensure nil
  :defer t
  :commands (woman)
  :config
  ;; Extract system manpath to guarantee `woman` locates modern Arch Linux manuals.
  (let ((path (cond
               ((executable-find "manpath")
                (split-string (string-trim (shell-command-to-string "manpath -q")) path-separator t))
               ((executable-find "man")
                (split-string (string-trim (shell-command-to-string "man --path")) path-separator t)))))
    (when path (setq woman-manpath path))))
#+end_src

** DONE Dired
Provides a visual directory editor and file manager for navigating the filesystem, copying, moving, and deleting files.
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(use-package dired
  :ensure nil
  :defer t
  :hook ((dired-mode . hl-line-mode))
  :custom
  ;; Suppress recursive operation prompts while maintaining baseline safety.
  (dired-recursive-copies 'always)
  (dired-recursive-deletes 'top)
  ;; DWIM target: suggest the other window's directory for copy/move operations.
  (dired-dwim-target t)
  ;; Prevent buffer bloat by killing old Dired buffers of the same directory.
  (dired-kill-when-opening-new-dired-buffer t)
  ;; Git Integration: Use `git mv` when renaming files in VC repositories.
  (dired-vc-rename-file t)
  ;; Prompt to create missing parent directories when copying/moving.
  (dired-create-destination-dirs 'ask)
  ;; Kill associated buffers when files are deleted via Dired.
  (dired-clean-up-buffers-too t)
  ;; Restrict C-s (isearch) to file names only when point is on a file.
  (dired-isearch-filenames 'dwim)
  ;; Emacs 29+: Enable drag-and-drop of files to external applications.
  (dired-mouse-drag-files t)
  ;; Defensive auto-revert: Revert local dirs, but strictly ignore TRAMP to prevent I/O hangs.
  (dired-auto-revert-buffer (lambda (dir)
                              (and (not (file-remote-p dir))
                                   (dired-buffer-stale-p))))
  :config
  ;; Arch Linux natively ships GNU coreutils. Establish baseline ls switches.
  (setq dired-listing-switches "-ahl -v --group-directories-first")

  ;; Enable `dired-find-alternate-file' to kill the current Dired buffer
  ;; when drilling down into files/directories, preventing buffer bloat.
  (put 'dired-find-alternate-file 'disabled nil)

  ;; Prevent auto-revert in virtual Dired buffers (e.g., find-dired, locate).
  ;; Reverting these buffers destroys the search results and throws errors.
  (defun ar/dired--no-revert-in-virtual-buffers-p (&rest _)
    "Return nil if the current buffer is a virtual Dired buffer."
    (not (eq revert-buffer-function #'dired-virtual-revert)))
  (advice-add #'dired-buffer-stale-p :before-while #'ar/dired--no-revert-in-virtual-buffers-p)

  ;; Surgically strip GNU-specific flags before `dired-readin` parses the directory.
  ;; Prevents blank screens over TRAMP when connecting to minimal containers (e.g., Alpine/BusyBox) or BSD servers.
  (defun ar/dired-strip-unsupported-ls-flags ()
    "Strip GNU-specific ls flags over TRAMP to prevent blank screens on minimal remote containers."
    (when (file-remote-p default-directory)
      (let ((switches dired-actual-switches))
        (setq switches (replace-regexp-in-string "--group-directories-first" "" switches))
        (setq switches (replace-regexp-in-string "-v" "" switches))
        ;; Clean up multiple spaces left behind after stripping
        (setq switches (replace-regexp-in-string "  +" " " (string-trim switches)))
        (setq-local dired-actual-switches switches))))
  (add-hook 'dired-before-readin-hook #'ar/dired-strip-unsupported-ls-flags)

  ;; Arch Linux / PGTK: Map common media and document extensions to xdg-open.
  (setq dired-guess-shell-alist-user
        '(("\\.pdf\\'" "xdg-open" "evince" "zathura")
          ("\\.\\(?:jpe?g\\|png\\|gif\\|svg\\|webp\\)\\'" "xdg-open" "imv")
          ("\\.\\(?:mp4\\|mkv\\|webm\\|mov\\)\\'" "xdg-open" "mpv")
          ("\\.\\(?:mp3\\|flac\\|wav\\|ogg\\)\\'" "xdg-open"))))
#+end_src


** TODO Dirvish
- [ ] Install dirvish from https://github.com/latiagertrutis/dirvish instead of melpa
- [ ] First print the finalized output of dired and dired extensions again to prompt
- [ ] Does wdired work in dirvish?

- [ ] Don't do anything from the TODO in the comments of the org source code block of dirvish
- [ ] Use no-littering principle to dirvish cache directory

Dirvish transforms Dired into a modern, Ranger-like file manager with live previews, emerge groups, and a dedicated sidebar.
*Architectural Note:* Dirvish in 2025/2026 suffers from a known multi-frame feedback loop (Issue #353) that causes severe UI flickering when =emacsclient= opens new frames. We apply a Doom Emacs =define-advice= patch to debounce the redisplay hook. Additionally, we hook into =persp-before-switch-functions= to cleanly quit Dirvish side-windows before switching workspaces, preventing Emacs from throwing errors when trying to delete dedicated windows.

#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(use-package dirvish
  :defer t
  :after dired
  :init
  ;; MAGIC: This makes ALL dired commands use Dirvish interfaces.
  ;; You never have to call `dirvish` explicitly; `dired` just becomes Dirvish.
  (dirvish-override-dired-mode)
  :custom
  ;; Store image/video preview caches in the Emacs directory to avoid polluting ~/.cache.
  (dirvish-cache-dir (expand-file-name "dirvish/" user-emacs-directory))

  ;; Layout configuration: (parent-depth preview-width file-list-width)
  (dirvish-default-layout '(1 0.11 0.55))
  (dirvish-layout-recipes
   '((0 0   0.4)   ; No parent, smaller preview
     (1 0.1 0.5)   ; Balanced layout
     (1 0.15 0.6))) ; More preview space

  ;; Quick access shortcuts for commonly visited directories.
  (dirvish-quick-access-entries
   `(("h" "~/" "Home")
     ("e" ,user-emacs-directory "Emacs")
     ("d" "~/Downloads/" "Downloads")
     ("D" "~/Documents/" "Documents")
     ("p" "~/projects/" "Projects")
     ("o" ,my/org-directory "Org")
     ("c" "~/.config/" "Config")
     ("t" "~/.local/share/Trash/files/" "Trash")))

  ;; Visual attributes rendered in the file list.
  (dirvish-attributes
   '(subtree-state nerd-icons collapse vc-state))

  ;; Doom-like UI: Enable custom headers and mode lines for Dirvish buffers.
  (dirvish-use-header-line t)
  (dirvish-use-mode-line t)
  (dirvish-header-line-height '(25 . 35))
  (dirvish-mode-line-height 25)
  (dirvish-header-line-format '(:left (path) :right (free-space)))
  (dirvish-mode-line-format
   '(:left (sort file-time " " file-size symlink)
           :right (omit yank index)))

  ;; Session behavior.
  ;; DOOM FIX: Set to 'open to prevent broken leftover buffers on errors.
  ;; If Dirvish crashes or is forcefully killed, setting this to `nil` leaves
  ;; ghost buffers in the background. `'open` ensures clean recovery.
  (dirvish-reuse-session 'open)
  (dirvish-hide-cursor t)
  (dirvish-hide-details '(dirvish dirvish-side))

  ;; Emerge groups: Automatically group files by type or recency.
  (dirvish-emerge-groups
   '(("Recent files" (predicate . recent-files-2h))
     ("Documents" (extensions "pdf" "tex" "bib" "epub"))
     ("Video" (extensions "mp4" "mkv" "webm"))
     ("Pictures" (extensions "jpg" "png" "svg" "gif"))
     ("Audio" (extensions "mp3" "flac" "wav" "ape"))
     ("Archives" (extensions "gz" "rar" "zip"))))

  ;; Preview dispatchers: Define how different file types are previewed.
  (dirvish-preview-dispatchers
   '(image gif video audio epub archive pdf dired))

  :config
  ;; Override the default mode-line format for the side-bar specifically.
  (setq dirvish-mode-line-format
        '(:left (sort symlink) :right (omit yank index)))

  ;; Open large directories (over 20000 files) asynchronously using the `fd` command
  ;; to prevent Emacs from freezing during directory read.
  (setq dirvish-large-directory-threshold 20000)
  (require 'dirvish-fd)
  (require 'dirvish-emerge)
  (require 'dirvish-side)

  ;; Side window configuration.
  ;; FIX: Syntax error corrected from original config. Set to left side to match width variable.
  (setq dirvish-side-width 30
        dirvish-side-follow-buffer-file t
        dirvish-side-display-alist '((side . left) (slot . -1) (window-width . 30)))

  ;; DOOM HACK: Prevent multi-frame feedback loop flickering (dirvish#353).
  ;; In Emacs 30+, opening multiple frames with Dirvish active causes a redisplay
  ;; feedback loop. This advice ensures the hook only fires for the selected window.
  (define-advice dirvish-pre-redisplay-h (:around (fn window) debounce-redisplay)
    (when (eq (frame-selected-window) window)
      (funcall fn window)))

  ;; TODO: modify this to work with projectile alternative
  ;; DOOM HACK: Clean up Dirvish before switching workspaces/projects.
  ;; If Dirvish is open in a dedicated side-window and you switch `persp-mode`
  ;; workspaces, Emacs will throw an error trying to delete the dedicated window.
  ;; This hook gracefully quits Dirvish before the workspace transition occurs.
  ;; (add-hook 'persp-before-switch-functions
  ;;           (lambda (&rest _)
  ;;             (when-let* ((dv (cl-loop for w in (window-list)
  ;;                                      if (window-dedicated-p w)
  ;;                                      if (with-current-buffer (window-buffer w) (dirvish-curr))
  ;;                                      return it)))
  ;;               (let (dirvish-reuse-session)
  ;;                 (with-selected-window (dv-root-window dv)
  ;;                   (dirvish-quit))))))


  )
#+end_src


** TODO Dired Extensions
These packages extend Dired's capabilities. =dired-x= provides advanced omitting and OS-integration, =diredfl= adds modern syntax highlighting (font-lock) to the directory listings, and =wdired= allows you to edit the Dired buffer like a text file to rename/move files in bulk.

#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(use-package dired-x
  :ensure nil
  :defer t
  ;; Enable omit mode to hide "uninteresting" files (compiled artifacts, VCS dirs).
  :hook (dired-mode . dired-omit-mode)
  :config
  (setq dired-omit-verbose nil
        ;; Regex defining which files to hide when `dired-omit-mode` is active.
        ;; Hides lockfiles, flycheck temp files, VCS directories, and compiled binaries.
        dired-omit-files
        (concat "^\\.?#\\|^\\.$\\|^\\.\\.$"
                "\\|^\\.DS_Store\\'"
                "\\|^flycheck_.*"
                "\\|^\\.project\\(?:ile\\)?\\'"
                "\\|^\\.\\(?:svn\\|git\\)\\'"
                "\\|^\\.ccls-cache\\'"
                "\\|\\(?:\\.js\\)?\\.meta\\'"
                "\\|\\.\\(?:elc\\|o\\|pyo\\|swp\\|class\\)\\'"))
  ;; Don't prompt for confirmation when killing buffers associated with deleted files.
  (setq dired-clean-confirm-killing-deleted-buffers nil))

(use-package diredfl
  ;; Apply syntax highlighting to both standard Dired and Dirvish's directory views.
  :hook
  ((dired-mode . diredfl-mode)
   (dirvish-directory-view-mode . diredfl-mode))
  :config
  ;; Make directory names bold for better visual distinction.
  (set-face-attribute 'diredfl-dir-name nil :bold t))

(use-package wdired
  :ensure nil
  :after dired
  :custom
  ;; Allow changing file permissions directly in the WDired buffer.
  (wdired-allow-to-change-permissions t)
  ;; Automatically create parent directories if you rename a file to a non-existent subfolder.
  (wdired-create-parent-directories t))
#+end_src

It is integrated with =nerd-icons= for visual flair and =ibuffer-projectile= to automatically group all open buffers by their respective Projectile projects.

(use-package ibuffer
  :ensure nil
  :defer t
  :init (setq ibuffer-filter-group-name-face '(:inherit (font-lock-string-face bold)))
  :config
  ;; DOOM FIX: Hide empty filter groups to keep the UI clean.
  ;; Without this, ibuffer-projectile will show empty headers for projects
  ;; that currently have no open buffers.
  (setq ibuffer-show-empty-filter-groups nil)

  ;; DOOM OPTIMIZATION: Redefine size column to show human-readable sizes.
  ;; The default byte count is hard to read; this converts it to K/M/G.
  (define-ibuffer-column size
    (:name "Size" :inline t :header-mouse-map ibuffer-size-header-map)
    (file-size-human-readable (buffer-size))))

;; Display icons for buffers based on their major mode or file extension.

** TODO Treemacs
Treemacs provides a persistent, mouse-and-keyboard-friendly file tree sidebar. We configure it to silently refresh in the background, integrate with Magit to update on Git commits, and use =nerd-icons= for crisp directory/file icons. Evil bindings are added for seamless Vim-style navigation within the tree.

#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(use-package treemacs
  :defer t
  :custom-face
  ;; Match the childframe border to the posframe border for visual consistency.
  (cfrs-border-color ((t (:inherit posframe-border))))
  ;; Make the root node stand out with a bold, constant-like face.
  (treemacs-root-face ((t (:inherit font-lock-constant-face :height 1.1 :weight bold))))
  :custom
  (treemacs-width 33)
  (treemacs-position 'left)
  ;; Prevent Treemacs from being selected by `other-window` (C-x o), keeping
  ;; the focus strictly on the editing and minibuffer windows.
  (treemacs-is-never-other-window t)
  (treemacs-space-between-root-nodes t)
  ;; Silent background operations to prevent echo-area spam.
  (treemacs-silent-refresh t)
  (treemacs-silent-filewatch t)
  ;; Delay before the sidebar follows the cursor to the current file.
  (treemacs-file-follow-delay 0.2)
  (treemacs-show-cursor nil)

  :config
  ;; Automatically follow the currently visited file in the tree.
  (treemacs-follow-mode t)
  ;; Watch the filesystem for external changes (e.g., git checkouts, file creation).
  (treemacs-filewatch-mode t)
  ;; Show a visual indicator in the fringe for the currently selected node.
  (treemacs-fringe-indicator-mode 'always)

  ;; DOOM FIX: Explicitly load the nerd-icons theme.
  ;; Without this, treemacs falls back to missing or ugly default ASCII icons.
  (use-package treemacs-nerd-icons
    :after treemacs
    :config (treemacs-load-theme "nerd-icons"))

  ;; Git integration configuration.
  ;; Deferred mode requires Python to parse git status asynchronously.
  ;; If Python is missing, fall back to simple (synchronous) mode.
  (when (and (executable-find "git")
             (executable-find "python3"))
    (treemacs-git-mode 'deferred)
    (setq treemacs-collapse-dirs 3))
  (when (and (executable-find "git")
             (not (executable-find "python3")))
    (treemacs-git-mode 'simple)
    (setq treemacs-collapse-dirs 0)))

;; Integrate Treemacs icons into standard Dired buffers.
(use-package treemacs-icons-dired
  :hook (dired-mode . treemacs-icons-dired-enable-once)
  :ensure t)

;; Magit integration: automatically refresh the sidebar after git operations.
(use-package treemacs-magit
  :after (treemacs magit)
  :hook ((magit-post-commit
          git-commit-post-finish
          magit-post-stage
          magit-post-unstage)
         . treemacs-magit--schedule-update))

;; Evil navigation in the sidebar with mouse interface.
(use-package treemacs-evil
  :after (treemacs evil)
  :config
  (evil-define-key 'normal treemacs-mode-map
    (kbd "TAB") #'treemacs-TAB-action
    (kbd "o")   #'treemacs-visit-node-ace
    (kbd "q")   #'treemacs-quit
    ;; Mouse interface for users who occasionally click the sidebar.
    [mouse-1] #'treemacs-single-click-expand-action
    [double-mouse-1] #'treemacs-doubleclick-action
    [drag-mouse-1] #'treemacs-dragleftclick-action))
#+end_src

(use-package magit
  :defer t
  :commands (magit-status
             magit-blame
             magit-log-buffer-file
             magit-stage-file
             magit-unstage-file
             magit-stage
             magit-unstage
             magit-commit
             magit-push-current-to-pushremote
             magit-pull-from-upstream
             magit-fetch
             magit-run-git
             magit-branch
             magit-merge
             magit-rebase
             magit-diff-unstaged
             magit-diff-staged
             magit-stash
             magit-dispatch)
  :custom
  ;; Display settings
  (magit-display-buffer-function #'magit-display-buffer-fullframe-status-v1)
  (magit-bury-buffer-function #'magit-restore-window-configuration)

  ;; Performance
  (magit-refresh-status-buffer t)
  (magit-refresh-verbose nil)
  (magit-git-executable "git")

  ;; Behavior
  (magit-save-repository-buffers 'dontask)
  (magit-commit-ask-to-stage 'stage)
  (magit-commit-show-diff t)
  (magit-revision-show-gravatars nil)

  ;; Diff settings
  (magit-diff-refine-hunk 'all)
  (magit-diff-paint-whitespace t)
  (magit-diff-highlight-trailing t)

  :config
  ;; Enable gravatars if desired (requires imagemagick)
  (when (and (display-graphic-p)
             (image-type-available-p 'imagemagick))
    (setq magit-revision-show-gravatars '("^Author:     " . "^Commit:     "))))

** TODO Diff-hl
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(use-package diff-hl
  :hook ((after-init . global-diff-hl-mode)
         (dired-mode . diff-hl-dired-mode))
  :hook (magit-pre-refresh . diff-hl-magit-pre-refresh)
  :hook (magit-post-refresh . diff-hl-magit-post-refresh)
  :init
  ;; Doom Emacs aesthetic: Thin vertical bars instead of blocks
  ;; We define custom bitmaps to match Doom's 'ui/vc-gutter' look
  (define-fringe-bitmap 'my-diff-hl-bmp-insert [224] nil nil '(center repeated))
  (define-fringe-bitmap 'my-diff-hl-bmp-modify [224] nil nil '(center repeated))
  (define-fringe-bitmap 'my-diff-hl-bmp-delete [128 192 224 240] nil nil 'bottom)

  :custom
  (diff-hl-draw-borders nil)
  (diff-hl-flydiff-delay 0.1) ;; Update immediately while typing

  :config
  ;; Enable real-time updates (equivalent to git-gutter+)
  (diff-hl-flydiff-mode 1)

  ;; Force the custom thin bitmaps defined in :init
  (setq diff-hl-fringe-bmp-function
        (lambda (type pos)
          (cond
           ((eq type 'delete) 'my-diff-hl-bmp-delete)
           ((eq type 'insert) 'my-diff-hl-bmp-insert)
           ((eq type 'change) 'my-diff-hl-bmp-modify)
           (t 'my-diff-hl-bmp-insert))))

  ;; Clean Tokyo Night colors (Foreground only, no ugly background blocks)
  (custom-set-faces
   '(diff-hl-insert ((t (:foreground "#9ece6a" :background unspecified))))
   '(diff-hl-change ((t (:foreground "#e0af68" :background unspecified))))
   '(diff-hl-delete ((t (:foreground "#f7768e" :background unspecified))))))
#+end_src

** TODO Git-Timemachine: Step Through File History
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(use-package git-timemachine
  :defer t
  :commands (git-timemachine git-timemachine-toggle)

  :init
  ;; CRITICAL FIX: Only configure native-comp if it's actually available
  (when (and (fboundp 'native-comp-available-p)
             (native-comp-available-p))
    (with-eval-after-load 'comp
      (when (boundp 'native-comp-deferred-compilation-deny-list)
        (add-to-list 'native-comp-deferred-compilation-deny-list "git-timemachine"))))

  :custom
  (git-timemachine-abbreviation-length 7)
  (git-timemachine-show-minibuffer-details t)

  :config
  (defun ar/git-timemachine-setup ()
    "Disable interfering modes when entering git-timemachine."
    (when (bound-and-true-p diff-hl-mode)
      (diff-hl-mode -1)
      (setq-local ar/diff-hl-was-active t))

    (when (bound-and-true-p eglot--managed-mode)
      (eglot-managed-mode -1)
      (setq-local ar/eglot-was-active t))

    (when (bound-and-true-p flymake-mode)
      (flymake-mode -1)
      (setq-local ar/flymake-was-active t))

    (read-only-mode 1))

  (defun ar/git-timemachine-teardown ()
    "Re-enable modes when exiting git-timemachine."
    (when (bound-and-true-p ar/diff-hl-was-active)
      (diff-hl-mode 1)
      (kill-local-variable 'ar/diff-hl-was-active))

    (when (bound-and-true-p ar/eglot-was-active)
      (eglot-ensure)
      (kill-local-variable 'ar/eglot-was-active))

    (when (bound-and-true-p ar/flymake-was-active)
      (flymake-mode 1)
      (kill-local-variable 'ar/flymake-was-active))

    ;; 4. Disable read-only
    (read-only-mode -1))

  (add-hook 'git-timemachine-mode-hook #'ar/git-timemachine-setup)
  (add-hook 'git-timemachine-mode-exit-hook #'ar/git-timemachine-teardown))
#+end_src

#+begin_src emacs-lisp
;; Define base directory with symlinks resolved
(defvar my/org-directory (file-truename (expand-file-name "~/org/"))
  "Base directory for all org files (symlinks resolved).")

;; Define all subdirectories that need to exist
(defvar my/org-subdirs
  '("notes"
    "notes/journal"
    "notes/literature"
    "gtd"
    "reviews"
    "downloads"
    "noter"
    "archive"
    "attachments"
    "backups")
  "List of subdirectories to create under `my/org-directory'.")

;; Helper function to get org subdirectory paths
(defun my/org-subdir (subdir)
  "Return full path for SUBDIR under `my/org-directory'."
  (expand-file-name subdir my/org-directory))

;; Helper function to ensure directory exists
(defun my/ensure-org-dir (subdir)
  "Ensure SUBDIR exists under `my/org-directory'.
Creates parent directories as needed. Returns the full path."
  (let ((dir (my/org-subdir subdir)))
    (unless (file-directory-p dir)
      (make-directory dir t)
      (message "Created directory: %s" dir))
    dir))

;; Helper function to create file with optional template content
(defun my/ensure-org-file (filename &optional template)
  "Ensure FILENAME exists in org directory with optional TEMPLATE content.
If the file does not exist, creates it. Returns the full path."
  (let ((filepath (expand-file-name filename my/org-directory)))
    (unless (file-exists-p filepath)
      ;; FIX: message moved outside with-temp-file so it fires after the file
      ;; is written, not as part of the buffer content evaluation.
      (with-temp-file filepath
        (when template
          (insert template)))
      (message "Created file: %s" filepath))
    filepath))

;; ============================================
;; CREATE ALL DIRECTORIES
;; ============================================
(mapc #'my/ensure-org-dir my/org-subdirs)

;; Journal file
(my/ensure-org-file "journal.org"
  "#+title: Journal
#+filetags: :journal:

,* Journal Entries

")

;; Habits tracking file
(my/ensure-org-file "habits.org"
  "#+title: Habits
#+filetags: :habit:

,* Daily Habits

,* Weekly Habits

,* Monthly Habits

")

;; Long-term goals file
(my/ensure-org-file "goals.org"
  "#+title: Goals
#+filetags: :goals:

,* Life Goals

,* This Year

,* This Quarter

,* This Month

")

;; Reading list and notes
(my/ensure-org-file "reading.org"
  "#+title: Reading List
#+filetags: :reading:

,* Currently Reading

,* To Read

,* Completed

")

;; Meeting notes
(my/ensure-org-file "meetings.org"
  "#+title: Meetings
#+filetags: :meeting:

,* Meetings

")


;; ============================================
;; DEFINE CONVENIENCE VARIABLES
;; ============================================
(defvar my/denote-directory      (my/org-subdir "notes/"))
(defvar my/org-gtd-directory     (my/org-subdir "gtd/"))
(defvar my/org-reviews-directory (my/org-subdir "reviews/"))
#+end_src

** TODO Better Font Faces
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(defun ar/org-font-setup ()
  ;; Replace list hyphen with dot
  (font-lock-add-keywords nil
                          '(("^ *\\([-]\\) "
                             (0 (prog1 () (compose-region (match-beginning 1) (match-end 1) "•"))))))

  ;; Set faces for heading levels
  (dolist (face '((org-level-1 . 1.13)
                  (org-level-2 . 1.11)
                  (org-level-3 . 1.10)
                  (org-level-4 . 1.07)
                  (org-level-5 . 1.05)
                  (org-level-6 . 1.03)
                  (org-level-7 . 1.02)
                  (org-level-8 . 1)))
    (set-face-attribute (car face) nil :font "JetBrainsMono Nerd Font" :weight 'bold :height (cdr face)))

  (custom-set-faces
   '(bold   ((t (:foreground "#e0af68" :weight bold))))
   '(italic ((t (:foreground "#bb9af7" :slant italic))))))
#+end_src

  :ensure nil

  ;; Core settings
  (org-modules nil)

  (org-log-done 'time)

** TODO Transient Template System
- [ ] Review transient code beforehand
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(with-eval-after-load 'org
  (defun ar/org-template-expand (str &optional mod)
    "Expand org template STR with optional modifier MOD."
    (let (text)
      (when (region-active-p)
        (setq text (buffer-substring (region-beginning) (region-end)))
        (delete-region (region-beginning) (region-end)))
      (insert str)
      (org-tempo-complete-tag)
      (when mod (insert mod) (forward-line))
      (when text (insert text))))

  (defun ar/org-insert-perl-tangled ()
    (interactive)
    (insert "#+HEADERS: :results output :exports both :shebang \"#!/usr/bin/env perl\"\n")
    (ar/org-template-expand "<s" "perl"))

  (defun ar/org-insert-ipython ()
    (interactive)
    (ar/org-template-expand "<s" "ipython :session :exports both :results raw drawer\n$0"))

  (defun ar/org-transient-title ()
    (concat
     (if (and (fboundp 'nerd-icons-sucicon) (display-graphic-p))
         (nerd-icons-sucicon "nf-custom-orgmode" :face 'transient-heading :v-adjust 0.02)
       "")
     (propertize " Org Template" 'face 'transient-heading)))

  (with-eval-after-load 'transient
    (transient-define-prefix ar/org-template-transient ()
      "Org Mode Structure Templates"
      [:description ar/org-transient-title

       ["Basic"
        ("a" "ascii"   (lambda () (interactive) (ar/org-template-expand "<a")))
        ("c" "center"  (lambda () (interactive) (ar/org-template-expand "<c")))
        ("C" "comment" (lambda () (interactive) (ar/org-template-expand "<C")))
        ("x" "example" (lambda () (interactive) (ar/org-template-expand "<e")))
        ("E" "export"  (lambda () (interactive) (ar/org-template-expand "<E")))
        ("h" "html"    (lambda () (interactive) (ar/org-template-expand "<h")))
        ("l" "latex"   (lambda () (interactive) (ar/org-template-expand "<l")))
        ("n" "note"    (lambda () (interactive) (ar/org-template-expand "<n")))
        ("o" "quote"   (lambda () (interactive) (ar/org-template-expand "<q")))
        ("v" "verse"   (lambda () (interactive) (ar/org-template-expand "<v")))]

       ["Head"
        ("i" "index"   (lambda () (interactive) (ar/org-template-expand "<i")))
        ("A" "ASCII"   (lambda () (interactive) (ar/org-template-expand "<A")))
        ("I" "INCLUDE" (lambda () (interactive) (ar/org-template-expand "<I")))
        ("H" "HTML"    (lambda () (interactive) (ar/org-template-expand "<H")))
        ("L" "LaTeX"   (lambda () (interactive) (ar/org-template-expand "<L")))]

       ["Source"
        ("s" "src"       (lambda () (interactive) (ar/org-template-expand "<s")))
        ("e" "emacs-lisp" (lambda () (interactive) (ar/org-template-expand "<s" "emacs-lisp")))
        ("y" "python"    (lambda () (interactive) (ar/org-template-expand "<s" "python")))
        ("w" "powershell" (lambda () (interactive) (ar/org-template-expand "<s" "powershell")))
        ("r" "ruby"      (lambda () (interactive) (ar/org-template-expand "<s" "ruby")))
        ("S" "sh"        (lambda () (interactive) (ar/org-template-expand "<s" "sh")))
        ("g" "golang"    (lambda () (interactive) (ar/org-template-expand "<s" "go :imports '(\"fmt\")")))]

       ["Misc"
        ("m" "mermaid"     (lambda () (interactive) (ar/org-template-expand "<s" "mermaid :file chart.png")))
         ("k" "kdl"         (lambda () (interactive) (ar/org-template-expand "<s" "kdl")))
        ("u" "plantuml"    (lambda () (interactive) (ar/org-template-expand "<s" "plantuml :file chart.png")))
         ("K" "kbd"         (lambda () (interactive) (ar/org-template-expand "<s" "kbd")))
        ("Z" "yaml"        (lambda () (interactive) (ar/org-template-expand "<s" "yaml")))
        ("Y" "ipython"     ar/org-insert-ipython)
        ("P" "Perl tangled" ar/org-insert-perl-tangled)
        ("<" "insert <"    self-insert-command)]]))

  (defun ar/org-smart-less-than ()
    "Smart less-than key: trigger transient menu or insert character."
    (interactive)
    (if (or (region-active-p) (looking-back "^[ \t]*" 1))
        (if (featurep 'transient)
            (ar/org-template-transient)
          (require 'transient)
          (ar/org-template-transient))
      (self-insert-command 1)))

  (define-key org-mode-map (kbd "<") #'ar/org-smart-less-than))
#+end_src

  ;; FIX: nerd-icons-codicon calls moved from :custom to :config.
  ;; In :custom, these execute via customize-set-variable at package-load

          (string-to-char (nerd-icons-codicon "nf-cod-check"))
          org-habit-today-glyph

** TODO Eldoc for Org Mode
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(defun ar/org-eldoc-breadcrumb (callback &rest _)
  "Show org heading breadcrumb path in eldoc."
  (when (derived-mode-p 'org-mode)
    (let* ((face-sep     '(:inherit shadow :height 0.8))
           (face-path    '(:inherit shadow))
           (face-current '(:inherit font-lock-keyword-face :weight bold))
           (sep-str (propertize " → " 'face face-sep)))
      (condition-case nil
          (unless (org-in-src-block-p)
            (cond
             ((or (eobp)
                  (save-excursion (forward-line 1) (eobp)))
              (funcall callback (propertize "Bottom Level" 'face face-path)))

             ((let ((path (ignore-errors (org-get-outline-path t))))
                (when path
                  (funcall callback
                           (mapconcat
                            (lambda (heading)
                              (if (string= heading (car (last path)))
                                  (propertize heading 'face face-current)
                                (propertize heading 'face face-path)))
                            path
                            sep-str)))))

             (t
              (funcall callback (propertize "Top Level" 'face face-path)))))
        (error nil)))))

(defun ar/org-eldoc-heading-info (callback &rest _)
  "Show TODO keyword, priority, and tags for current heading."
  (when (derived-mode-p 'org-mode)
    (condition-case nil
        (let* ((element (org-element-at-point))
               (type (org-element-type element)))
          (when (eq type 'headline)
            (let* ((todo     (org-element-property :todo-keyword element))
                   (priority (org-element-property :priority element))
                   (tags     (org-element-property :tags element))
                   (info-parts '()))
              (when todo
                (push (propertize todo 'face (org-get-todo-face todo))
                      info-parts))
              (when priority
                (push (propertize (format "[#%c]" priority) 'face
                                  (org-get-priority-face priority))
                      info-parts))
              (when tags
                (push (propertize (concat ":" (string-join tags ":") ":")
                                  'face 'org-tag)
                      info-parts))
              (when info-parts
                (funcall callback (string-join (nreverse info-parts) " "))))))
      (error nil))))

(defun ar/org-eldoc-timestamps (callback &rest _)
  "Show scheduling info (SCHEDULED, DEADLINE, CLOSED) for current heading."
  (when (derived-mode-p 'org-mode)
    (condition-case nil
        (let* ((element (org-element-at-point))
               (type (org-element-type element)))
          (when (eq type 'headline)
            (let* ((scheduled (org-element-property :scheduled element))
                   (deadline  (org-element-property :deadline  element))
                   (closed    (org-element-property :closed    element))
                   (timestamps '()))
              (when scheduled
                (push (concat
                       (propertize "SCHEDULED: " 'face 'org-special-keyword)
                       (org-element-property :raw-value scheduled))
                      timestamps))
              (when deadline
                (push (concat
                       (propertize "DEADLINE: " 'face 'org-special-keyword)
                       (org-element-property :raw-value deadline))
                      timestamps))
              (when closed
                (push (concat
                       (propertize "CLOSED: " 'face 'org-special-keyword)
                       (org-element-property :raw-value closed))
                      timestamps))
              (when timestamps
                (funcall callback (string-join (nreverse timestamps) " │ "))))))
      (error nil))))

(defun ar/org-eldoc-link (callback &rest _)
  "Show link type and target."
  (when (derived-mode-p 'org-mode)
    (condition-case nil
        (when-let ((context (org-element-context)))
          (when (eq (org-element-type context) 'link)
            (let* ((type (org-element-property :type context))
                   (path (org-element-property :path context))
                   (desc (when (org-element-property :contents-begin context)
                           (buffer-substring-no-properties
                            (org-element-property :contents-begin context)
                            (org-element-property :contents-end context)))))
              (when (and type path)
                (funcall callback
                         (concat
                          (propertize "Link: " 'face 'bold)
                          (propertize (concat type ":" path) 'face 'link)
                          (when desc
                            (format " [%s]"
                                    (propertize desc 'face 'font-lock-doc-face)))))))))
      (error nil))))

(defun ar/org-eldoc-table (callback &rest _)
  "Show table cell position and formula information."
  (when (and (derived-mode-p 'org-mode)
             (org-at-table-p)
             (not (org-at-table.el-p)))
    (condition-case nil
        (let* ((col (org-table-current-column))
               (row (save-excursion
                      (org-table-goto-line 1)
                      (count-lines (org-table-begin) (line-beginning-position))))
               (formula (org-table-get-formula col)))
          (funcall callback
                   (concat
                    (propertize (format "Cell[%d,%d]" row col)
                                'face 'font-lock-keyword-face)
                    (when (and formula (not (string-empty-p formula)))
                      (format " Formula: %s"
                              (propertize formula 'face 'font-lock-function-name-face))))))
      (error nil))))

(defun ar/org-eldoc-drawer (callback &rest _)
  "Show current drawer name."
  (when (derived-mode-p 'org-mode)
    (condition-case nil
        (save-excursion
          (beginning-of-line)
          (when (looking-at org-drawer-regexp)
            (let ((drawer (match-string 1)))
              (funcall callback
                       (propertize (format "Drawer: :%s:" drawer)
                                   'face 'org-drawer)))))
      (error nil))))

(defun ar/org-eldoc-property (callback &rest _)
  "Show property at point."
  (when (derived-mode-p 'org-mode)
    (condition-case nil
        (let ((context (org-element-context)))
          (when (eq (org-element-type context) 'node-property)
            (let ((key   (org-element-property :key   context))
                  (value (org-element-property :value context)))
              (funcall callback
                       (format "%s: %s"
                               (propertize key   'face 'org-special-keyword)
                               (propertize value 'face 'font-lock-string-face))))))
      (error nil))))

(defun ar/org-eldoc-clock (callback &rest _)
  "Show currently clocked item if any."
  (when (and (derived-mode-p 'org-mode)
             (featurep 'org-clock)
             (bound-and-true-p org-clock-current-task))
    (condition-case nil
        (funcall callback
                 (format "%s %s"
                         (propertize "CLOCKING:" 'face 'org-special-keyword)
                         (propertize org-clock-current-task 'face 'org-clock-overlay)))
      (error nil))))

;; On-demand functions (not in eldoc rotation)
(defun ar/org-show-statistics ()
  "Show statistics about current subtree (word count, task count)."
  (interactive)
  (when (derived-mode-p 'org-mode)
    (condition-case nil
        (let* ((element (org-element-at-point))
               (type (org-element-type element)))
          (if (eq type 'headline)
              (save-excursion
                (org-narrow-to-subtree)
                (let* ((words (count-words (point-min) (point-max)))
                       ;; FIX: org-map-entries requires a callable as its first
                       ;; argument. The original passed bare `t', which throws
                       ;; (wrong-type-argument functionp t) at runtime.
                       (tasks (length (org-map-entries
                                       (lambda () t)
                                       "/+TODO|DONE|NEXT|WAIT|HOLD" 'tree)))
                       (done  (length (org-map-entries
                                       (lambda () t)
                                       "/+DONE" 'tree))))
                  (widen)
                  (message (concat
                            (when (> words 0)
                              (format "%s words"
                                      (propertize (number-to-string words)
                                                  'face 'font-lock-constant-face)))
                            (when (and (> words 0) (> tasks 0)) " │ ")
                            (when (> tasks 0)
                              (format "Tasks: %s/%s"
                                      (propertize (number-to-string done)
                                                  'face 'org-done)
                                      (propertize (number-to-string tasks)
                                                  'face 'org-todo)))))))
            (message "Not in an Org heading")))
      (error (message "Failed to calculate statistics")))))

(defun ar/org-show-src-block-info ()
  "Show source block language and important parameters."
  (interactive)
  (when (and (derived-mode-p 'org-mode)
             (org-in-src-block-p))
    (condition-case nil
        (save-excursion
          (let* ((element    (org-element-at-point))
                 (lang       (org-element-property :language   element))
                 (switches   (org-element-property :switches   element))
                 (parameters (org-element-property :parameters element)))
            (when lang
              (message (concat
                        (propertize "Source: " 'face 'bold)
                        (propertize lang 'face 'font-lock-keyword-face)
                        (when switches
                          (format " %s" (propertize switches 'face 'font-lock-comment-face)))
                        (when (and parameters (not (string-empty-p parameters)))
                          (format " [%s]"
                                  (propertize parameters 'face 'font-lock-comment-face))))))))
      (error (message "Not in a source block or failed to retrieve info")))))

;; Keybindings for on-demand functions
(with-eval-after-load 'org
  (define-key org-mode-map (kbd "C-c i s") #'ar/org-show-statistics)
  (define-key org-mode-map (kbd "C-c i b") #'ar/org-show-src-block-info))

;; Register eldoc functions for org-mode
(add-hook 'org-mode-hook
          (lambda ()
            (setq-local eldoc-documentation-strategy 'eldoc-documentation-compose)
            (add-hook 'eldoc-documentation-functions #'ar/org-eldoc-clock          -100 t)
            (add-hook 'eldoc-documentation-functions #'ar/org-eldoc-link            -80 t)
            (add-hook 'eldoc-documentation-functions #'ar/org-eldoc-table           -70 t)
            (add-hook 'eldoc-documentation-functions #'ar/org-eldoc-property        -60 t)
            (add-hook 'eldoc-documentation-functions #'ar/org-eldoc-drawer          -50 t)
            (add-hook 'eldoc-documentation-functions #'ar/org-eldoc-heading-info    -40 t)
            (add-hook 'eldoc-documentation-functions #'ar/org-eldoc-timestamps      -30 t)
            (add-hook 'eldoc-documentation-functions #'ar/org-eldoc-breadcrumb      -10 t)))
#+end_src

#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(use-package org-gtd
  :defer t
  :after org
  :commands (org-gtd-capture
             org-gtd-process-inbox
             org-gtd-engage
             org-gtd-show-all-next
             org-gtd-show-stuck-projects
             org-gtd-organize)
  :init

  (setq org-gtd-update-ack "4.0.0")
  ;; Set GTD directory before load so org-gtd can locate its files.
  (setq org-gtd-directory my/org-gtd-directory)
  (my/ensure-org-dir "gtd")

  :custom
  (org-todo-keywords
   '((sequence "TODO(t)" "NEXT(n)" "PROG(p)" "WAIT(w@/!)" "|" "DONE(d!)" "CANCEL(c@)")
     (sequence "PLAN(P)" "ACTIVE(A)" "PAUSED(x)" "|" "ACHIEVED(a)" "DROPPED(D)")
     ;; GTD-specific keywords (used only in ~/org/gtd/ files)
     (sequence "INBOX(i)" "GTD-NEXT(N)" "GTD-WAIT(W@/!)" "|" "GTD-DONE(D!)" "GTD-CNCL(C@)")))

  ;; Map GTD semantic states to the keywords above
  (org-gtd-keyword-mapping
   '((todo     . "INBOX")
     (next     . "GTD-NEXT")
     (wait     . "GTD-WAIT")
     (done     . "GTD-DONE")
     (canceled . "GTD-CNCL")))

  ;; FIX: Removed (org-gtd-mode nil) from :custom. You cannot deactivate a
  ;; minor mode via customize-set-variable. The mode is off by default; the
  ;; idle timer in :config handles deferred activation.

  :config
  ;; org-edna is a required dependency; activate its minor mode here.
  (org-edna-mode 1)

  ;; Authoritative definition of org-agenda-files — kept in one place only.
  (setq org-agenda-files
        (list my/org-directory
              my/org-gtd-directory))

  ;; Performance: defer full mode activation until Emacs is idle
  (run-with-idle-timer 30 nil
    (lambda ()
      (when (and (featurep 'org-gtd)
                 (not (bound-and-true-p org-gtd-mode)))
        (org-gtd-mode 1)))))
#+end_src

** TODO Weekly Review
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(defun ar/weekly-review ()
  "Open or create this week's Denote review note, pre-populated with stats."
  (interactive)
  (require 'denote)
  (let* ((week   (format-time-string "%Y-W%V"))
         (regexp (concat "__review.*" (regexp-quote week)))
         (files  (denote-directory-files regexp)))
    (if files
        (find-file (car files))
      ;; Count pending tasks in GTD inbox
      (let* ((inbox-file  (expand-file-name "gtd/inbox.org" my/org-directory))
             (inbox-count
              (if (file-exists-p inbox-file)
                  (with-temp-buffer
                    (insert-file-contents inbox-file)
                    (count-matches "^\\* INBOX " (point-min) (point-max)))
                0))
             ;; Count GTD-NEXT actions
             (next-count
              (length (org-map-entries
                       (lambda () t)
                       "TODO=\"GTD-NEXT\""
                       (list my/org-gtd-directory))))
             ;; Count Denote notes created in the last 7 days
             (week-start (format-time-string "%Y%m%d"
                           (time-subtract (current-time)
                                          (seconds-to-time (* 7 24 60 60)))))
             (new-notes
              (length (seq-filter
                       (lambda (f)
                         (let ((id (denote-retrieve-filename-identifier f)))
                           (and id (string>= (substring id 0 8) week-start))))
                       (denote-directory-files)))))
        (denote (concat "Weekly Review " week) '("review"))
        (goto-char (point-max))
        (insert
         (concat
          "\n* GTD Health Check\n\n"
          (format "- Inbox items: *%d* %s\n"
                  inbox-count
                  (if (> inbox-count 10) "⚠ PROCESS URGENTLY" "✓"))
          (format "- GTD-NEXT actions: *%d*\n"
                  next-count)
          (format "- New Denote notes this week: *%d*\n\n" new-notes)
          "* Review Checklist\n\n"
          "** GTD Review\n"
          "- [ ] Process GTD inbox to zero\n"
          "- [ ] Review all GTD-WAIT items\n"
          "- [ ] Confirm each project has a NEXT action\n"
          "- [ ] Review calendar (past week + next 2 weeks)\n"
          "- [ ] Review Someday/Maybe\n\n"
          "** Notes Review\n"
          "- [ ] Review notes created this week\n"
          "- [ ] Identify missing connections\n"
          "- [ ] Rename/re-keyword any stale notes\n\n"
          "* Reflections\n\n"
          "** What Went Well?\n\n\n"
          "** What Could Improve?\n\n\n"
          "** Key Insights This Week\n\n\n"
          "** Next Week's Focus\n\n\n"))))))
#+end_src
